<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user's initial goal was to fix a series of bugs in a React Native mobile application named SpiritSongs (or Gracefy).

**Original Requirements:**
*   Fix app crashes on various screens (Library, Search, Playlist, Downloads, Liked Songs).
*   Fix the Bible section, which was showing a blank screen.
*   Fix the Mafundisho na katekesi section, which wasn't loading audio content.
*   Address the very slow application load time.
*   Ensure the hero section displays content images correctly.

**Subsequent Requirements:**
*   Implement a full analytics and reporting system for the admin panel, including user listening history, revenue analytics, content performance, and navigation tracking.
*   Fix the admin panel's Transactions and Settings pages, which were failing to load.
*   After multiple attempts to fix crashes, the user requested a complete redevelopment from scratch of the Library, Liked Songs, Downloaded Songs, User Playlist, and Search sections.
*   After the redevelopment, the user reported that core functionalities like downloading, liking songs, and playlist management were broken.

**User's preferred language**: English

**what currently exists?**
The project is a full-stack application with a React Native mobile app, a React-based web admin panel, and a Python/Flask backend using MongoDB. The agent has made extensive changes across the entire stack. Many backend APIs for analytics and admin functionality have been added. On the mobile app, several critical screens (, , ) were completely rewritten to solve persistent crashing issues. While the rewrite fixed the crashes, it broke essential user features like downloading songs, liking songs, and managing playlists.
